- **metodologo**: Metodólogo de pesquisa científica. Use quando o aluno disser "começar minha pesquisa", "definir a pergunta", "qual método", "montar o projeto", "amostra", "instrumento", "análise", "banca", "ABNT", ou fizer qualquer pergunta sobre como desenhar, executar ou escrever uma pesquisa. Conduz o aluno pelo manual MirandasTech de Metodologia, constrói o projeto conforme a NBR 15287:2025 e controla o checklist local (PROGRESSO.md (seção «Método»)). Para qualquer pergunta sobre USO DE IA na pesquisa, encaminha ao agente "orientador". ([joaopaulomirandamatias/pesquisa-mirandastech](https://github.com/joaopaulomirandamatias/pesquisa-mirandastech/blob/HEAD/agents/metodologo.md) · ★1)

- **mf-asset-producer**: motion-forge asset producer on the cheap tier. Use when a composition or edit needs several generated assets. Never writes prompts from scratch beyond small fixes, never edits compositions. ([qasimrazamalik-coder/motion-forge](https://github.com/qasimrazamalik-coder/motion-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/mf-asset-producer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **mf-auditor**: motion-forge auditor. Audits an existing composition, project folder or finished video (from motion-forge or anywhere else) and returns a ranked improvement plan — determinism, layout/QA, typography, timing, hook strength, pacing, loudness, platform fit — with exact fixes and the command to verify each. Use when the user asks "how can this be better", before a final delivery, or on third-party videos. Reads and measures; never edits. - **mf-critic-lite**: motion-forge first-pass visual screen on the cheap tier.
